# Recipes

Generated by `Tools/SdbDocs` from a build-1962 `clientdb.sd2` (db patch prod-1962, built 2016-05-05).

`dbitems::Blueprints` holds 9228 rows and `dbitems::Blueprint_Items` holds their
ingredients. 5763 blueprints name an item that still exists and are listed here;
3465 point at an output with no English name. 853 of the listed ones have no
ingredient rows at all.

v1.6 switched crafting off in January 2016 and it never came back, so none of this is reachable in
the client PIN targets. The data survived the migration intact -- see
[Thumping and Crafting](../Thumping-And-Crafting.md) for what turning it back on would take, and
[Restoration](../../Restoration.md) for why a curated subset of these is a complete answer rather
than a compromise.

`Research` is the blueprint that has to be researched first, where there is one. Blueprint types are
the raw `blueprint_type` values; what each one meant is not recorded anywhere that survives, so they
are grouped rather than named.
